 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00009 - PYTHON
============================================================

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_09.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "ebee6be46b548b7f1c8a9370dbafbe5d2562493086d7f470b1bb39516aa7ddee"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00010 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_10(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_10(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_06.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "22c0b90eb73266f02f58b775ec453542e3b61f858caf668d7290e36fd052f3f1"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00040 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_11(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_09(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_03.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "55ee12c24097f1ef4dcaa597e180ea81e891ec9fd64343b21e4e8097c6ddc9a8"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00070 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_12(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_08(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_00.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "20985dfd722a14b08ea1e1d9fd2e6365d9adbcad59054e9895739c90c2f0b1dd"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00100 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_13(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_07(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_08.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "3dcdc8d8d425cacf84383a0863617b0a5bb0a0c022986495bf7da0ad3f6d0f6a"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00130 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_14(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_06(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_21(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_05.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "cd90d4aec1eb85fb1ff4d0d9b5f63a82f8f628f2fcc443e6407af9630cfae103"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_01(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_20(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_02.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "fbb095a079e0aa992d51deb2c4c8ef6e51ac98dc58124b43e0cb893ad1cb473e"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00204 - PYTHON

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_06.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "f2c85f254582836a8df24007662c09ca3d4cd33482cfa5249893ed73a92347c7"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00205 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_02(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_19(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_03.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "7125d01220c20783c0aef82748504d80b04354c72d04665d5fac527bf2d59e83"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00235 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_03(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_18(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_00.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "dcb0527a499d7597ce5340457cbe9c5d07f6397025e415381c9d7b3d801ca836"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00265 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_04(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_17(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_08.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "f02d16cec273697a0a691a13b8f105fc6cf2501560cbc896fa99112ec253523e"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00295 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_05(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_16(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_05.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "80679db46b8abd487c365ec20489201dcbbec1d0ed00fa93d4e76646afbab131"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00325 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_06(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_15(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_02.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "cc7cc505e145491a0573e84e4cb8dfc9e54822fddb957ed034523d0f0987ab07"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00355 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_07(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_14(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_10.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "91af4b73a9530e2f9057d9eb40849431f4dd7628f8bc713a68d2b0c773f96492"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00385 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_08(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_13(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

MODEL = Path("/models/ninfer-custom/model_07.ninfer")
EXPECTED = "688410dedc03e0b142f9d40b6d6f03546b12dc7808cbb0fb7391f426caab7184"

def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

result = {
    "artifact": str(MODEL),
    "expected_sha256": EXPECTED,
    "actual_sha256": sha256(MODEL) if MODEL.exists() else None,
    "context": 131072,
    "kv_capacity": 131072,
    "prefill_chunk": 896,
    "draft_tokens": 3,
}

print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

============================================================
 WORKFLOW SEGMENT 00415 - C++ CUDA
============================================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <stdexcept>

struct RuntimeConfig {
    std::uint32_t max_context = 131072;
    std::uint32_t kv_capacity = 131072;
    std::uint32_t prefill_chunk = 896;
    std::uint32_t draft_tokens = 3;
    bool use_cuda_graph = false;
};

__global__ void gather_embedding_09(
    const std::uint16_t* token_ids,
    const std::uint8_t* weights,
    float* output,
    int token_count) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    if (row < token_count && lane < 128) {
        output[row * 128 + lane] =
            static_cast<float>(weights[token_ids[row] * 128 + lane]);
    }
}

void validate_runtime_12(const RuntimeConfig& cfg) {
    if (cfg.max_context < cfg.prefill_chunk) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid runtime geometry");
    }
}

context. Diversity should be measured afterward as a diagnostic rather than
being the primary construction target.
